ABOUT CR POWER
關於華潤電力

China Resources Power Holdings Company Limited (the “Company”           華潤電力控股有限公司（「本公司」或「華
or “CR Power”), together with its subsidiaries (the “Group”) mainly     潤 電 力」）及 其 附 屬 公 司（「本 集 團」）主 要
invests, develops, operates and manages wind farms, photovoltaic        在中華人民共和國（「中國」）投資、開發、
power plants, hydroelectric power plants and other clean and            運營和管理風電場、光伏電站、水電站及
renewable energy projects and coal-fired power plants in the            其他清潔及可再生能源項目和燃煤發電
People’s Republic of China (“China” or the “PRC”). Its business also    廠。業務還涉及分佈式能源、售電、智慧
covers distributed energy, power sales, intelligent energy, coal        能源及煤炭等領域。
mining, and other areas.

As at 30 June 2025, CR Power had 50 coal-fired power plants, 209        於 2025 年 6 月 30 日，華 潤 電 力 旗 下 運 營
wind farms, 205 photovoltaic power plants, 20 hydroelectric power       50 座 燃 煤 發 電 廠、209 座 風 電 場、205 座
plants and 6 gas-fired power plants in commercial operations. Its       光伏電站、20 座水電站和 6 座燃氣發電廠。
total attributable grid-connected installed capacity was 78,094 MW,     本公司的合計權益併網裝機容量為 78,094
with 26.4% of its capacity located in Central China, 22.1% in           兆 瓦，其 中 26.4% 位 於 華 中 地 區，22.1%
Eastern China, 14.0% in Southern China, 13.6% in Northern China,        位 於 華 東 地 區，14.0% 位 於 華 南 地 區，
11.4% in Northwestern China, 6.3% in Southwestern China, 6.1%           13.6% 位於華北地區，11.4% 位於西北地
in Northeastern China and 0.1% in Hong Kong Special                     區，6.3% 位 於 西 南 地 區，6.1% 位 於 東 北
Administrative Region of China (“Hong Kong”) and overseas. Its          地 區，以 及 0.1% 位 於 中 國 香 港 特 別 行 政
attributable grid-connected installed capacity in wind, hydroelectric   區（「香港」   ）及海外。風電、水電及光伏發
and photovoltaic power combined, amounted to 38,955 MW,                 電權益併網裝機容量合共 38,955 兆瓦，佔
accounting for approximately 49.9% of its total attributable grid-      總權益併網裝機容量約 49.9%。
connected installed capacity.

CR Power has been selected, for 6 consecutive years, as a constituent   自 2020 年 9 月 7 日 起，華 潤 電 力 已 連 續
of the Hang Seng ESG 50 Index and the Hang Seng Corporate               6 年入選恒生 ESG 50 指數成份股和恒生可
Sustainability Benchmark Index (“HSSUSB”) since 7 September 2020.       持續發展企業基準指數。

CR Power has always adhered to the concept of sustainable               華潤電力始終秉持可持續發展理念，將落
development and integrated the implementation of environmental,         實環境、社會和治理責任融入公司戰略和
social and governance responsibilities into the Company’s strategies    業 務 運 作，本 公 司 連 續 15 年 發 佈 可 持 續
and operations. The Company has been publishing sustainability          發展報告，不斷審視自身的表現和不足，
reports for 15 consecutive years, constantly reviews its own            努力提高踐行可持續發展的能力，為企業
performance and shortcomings, and strives to improve its sustainable    長遠的價值創造奠定了堅實的基礎。連續
development practices, laying a solid foundation for creating long-     6 年入選恒生 ESG 50 指數以及恒生可持續
term value for the Company. CR Power’s inclusion into the Hang          發展企業基準指數，彰顯了華潤電力在環
Seng ESG 50 Index and HSSUSB for 6 consecutive years demonstrates       境、社會及企業管治三個範疇表現卓越，
its excellence in the three aspects of environment, social and          反映了資本市場對本公司可持續發展工
governance, and reflects the recognition on the sustainable             作的認可。
development efforts of the Company by the capital markets.

CR Power has also been selected as a constituent in the Hang Seng       自 2023 年 6 月 5 日起，華潤電力亦被納入
Index since 5 June 2023. The Company’s efforts towards strategic        恒生指數成份股，充分顯示了資本市場對
transformation and innovative development are highly recognised by      於本公司轉型和創新發展的高度認可。
the capital markets.

Seek for low-carbon innovation transformation                              探索低碳創新轉型
Adhering to the innovation-driven concept, by combining its rich           本集團堅持秉承創新驅動理念，結合自身
experience and advanced technologies, the Group enhances the               豐富經驗和先進技術，提高產業質效、培
quality and efficiency of the industry and cultivates the future           育未來生態，為企業構建新質生產力提供
ecology, for the purpose of providing solid support for the                堅實支撐。我們充分鼓勵碳捕集、利用與
construction of new-quality productivity. We fully encourage the           封 存 (CCUS) 等 低 碳 技 術 發 展，積 極 探 索
development of low-carbon technologies such as carbon capture,             智慧儲能、虛擬電廠等新領域，促進碳減
utilization and storage (CCUS), actively explore new fields such           排目標實施，推進創新成果轉化，提升綜
as smart energy storage and virtual power plants, promote the              合能源服務能力，在低碳創新領域切實發
implementation of carbon reduction targets, drive the transformation       揮華潤價值。
of innovative achievements, improve comprehensive energy service
capabilities, and effectively leverage the value of China Resources in
the field of low-carbon innovation.

The Group explicitly includes carbon emission reduction and                本集團將碳減排、可再生能源發展等明確
renewable energy development in the performance contracts of the           列入高管團隊業績合同，關鍵業績指標包
senior management, with the key performance indicators including,          括可再生能源權益裝機佔比、新增可再生
among others, the proportion of attributable generation capacity of        能源併網容量、供電碳排放強度等，並根
renewable energy, new grid-connected capacity of renewable energy          據實際完成情況考核業績表現。
and the intensity of carbon emissions in power supply, for which
the appraisal of results performance will be conducted based on the
actual completion status.

Basis of Preparation of Financial Information and                        財務信息的編製基準及主要會計政策
Principal Accounting Policies
This condensed consolidated financial information has been prepared      本簡明合併財務信息已根據香港會計師
in accordance with Hong Kong Accounting Standard 34 (“HKAS               公會頒佈的香港會計準則第 34 號（「香港
34”) “Interim Financial Reporting” as issued by the Hong Kong            會計準則第 34 號」）「中期財務報告」及香
Institute of Certified Public Accountants (“HKICPA”) as well as with     港聯合交易所有限公司證券上市規則（「上
the applicable disclosure requirements of Appendix D2 to the Rules       市規則」）附錄 D2 的適用披露規定而編製。
Governing the Listing of Securities on The Stock Exchange of Hong
Kong Limited (the “Listing Rules”).

As at 30 June 2025, the Group had net current liabilities of             本 集 團 於 2025 年 6 月 30 日 擁 有 流 動 負 債
HK$45,524 million. The Directors are of the opinion that, taking into    淨 額 455.24 億 港 元。董 事 認 為，經 考 慮
account the current operation of the Group as well as the unutilised     本集團當前的經營以及本集團可供動用
banking facilities available to the Group, the Group has sufficient      的銀行授信，本集團有足夠營運資金悉數
working capital to enable it to meet in full its financial obligations   履行其由簡明合併財務狀況表日期起計
as and when they fall due in the coming twelve months from the           未來十二個月到期的財務責任。因此，該
date of the condensed consolidated statement of financial position.      等簡明合併中期財務信息已按持續經營
Therefore, such condensed consolidated interim financial information     基準編製。
has been prepared on a going concern basis.

Changes in accounting policies and disclosures                           會計政策的變動及披露
The condensed consolidated financial information has been prepared       除特定金融工具以公允價值計量外，簡明
on the historical cost basis except for certain financial instruments,   合併財務信息按歷史成本為基礎編製。
which are measured at fair values.

The accounting policies and methods of computation used in the           截 至 2025 年 6 月 30 日 止 六 個 月 的 簡 明 合
condensed consolidated financial information for the six months          併財務信息所採用的會計政策及計算方
ended 30 June 2025 is the same as those presented in the Group’s         法與本集團截至 2024 年 12 月 31 日止年度
annual financial statements for the year ended 31 December 2024.         之年度財務報表所呈列者相同。

The Group has not applied the new and amendments to HKFRS                本集團並未應用由香港會計師公會發佈
Accounting Standards that have been issued by the HKICPA but are         但尚未生效的新訂香港財務報告準則及
not yet effective.                                                       其修訂本。

Capital structure management                                               資本結構管理
The Group and the Company manage its capital structure to ensure           本集團及本公司資本結構管理的宗旨乃
that entities in the Group will be able to continue as a going concern     確保本集團內各實體將可以持續方式經
while maximising the return to Shareholders through optimising the         營，同時透過優化債項及股本結構，為股
debt and equity structures. The overall strategies of the Group and        東帶來最大回報。本集團及本公司整體策
the Company remain unchanged as those adopted in the previous              略與過往年度一樣維持不變。
years.

The capital structure of the Group consists of net debts (including        本集團資本結構包括淨負債（其中包括長
long-term and short-term bank borrowings, corporate bonds,                 短期銀行借貸、公司債券、中期票據、關
medium-term notes and loans from related parties), cash and cash           聯方借貸）、現金及現金等價物、已抵押
equivalents, pledged and restricted bank deposits and total equity.        及受限制銀行存款及總權益。

The Directors review the capital structure on a regular basis, including   董事定期檢討資本結構，包括資本成本及
the cost of capital and the risks associated with each class of capital.   與每一類別資本有關的風險。本集團透過
The Group will balance its overall capital structure through payment       派付股息、發行新債或償還現有負債，平
of dividends and the issue of new debts or the repayment of existing       衡整體資本結構。
debts.

  the Group’s overall strategic objectives in the PRC, following          退 出 中 國 市 場 以 調 整 其 投 資 策 略，
  the decision made by the vendor who wishes to exit the PRC              收購潤捷之目的符合本集團在中國
  market as to align its investment strategy. The acquisition was         的 整 體 戰 略 目 標。該 收 購 採 用 收 購

34. FAIR VALUE MEASUREMENTS OF                                                  34. 金融工具的公允價值計量
    FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS
      Fair value measurements                                                     公允價值計量
      The fair values of these financial assets are determined (in                該等金融資產的公允價值已予釐定
      particular, the valuation technique(s) and inputs used), as well           （尤其是所使用的估值技術及輸入數
      as the level of the fair value hierarchy into which the fair value          據），以及公允價值計量的公允價值
      measurements are categorised (Levels 1 to 3) based on the                   層級（第一至三級）乃根據公允價值
      degree to which the inputs to the fair value measurements is                計量的輸入數據的可觀察程度而釐定。
      observable.

      •       Level 1 fair value measurements are based on quoted                 •   第一級公允價值計量乃基於相
              prices (unadjusted) in active market for identical assets or            同資產或負債在活躍市場的報
              liabilities;                                                            價（未經調整）；

      •       Level 2 fair value measurements are those derived from              •    第二級公允價值計量來自資產
              inputs other than quoted prices included within Level 1                  或負債的直接（即價格）或間接
              that are observable for the asset or liability, either directly         （即源自價格）可觀察輸入數據
              (i.e. as prices) or indirectly (i.e. derived from prices);              （第一級包括的報價除外）；

      •       Level 3 fair value measurements are those derived from              •   第三級公允價值計量指來自估
              valuation techniques that include inputs for the asset or               值 技 術 的 計 量，包 括 並 非 基 於
              liability that are not based on observable market data                  可觀察市場數據釐定的資產或
              (unobservable inputs).                                                  負債的輸入數據（不可觀察輸入
                                                                                      數據）。

35. EVENTS AFTER THE END OF THE                                       35. 報告期末後事項
    REPORTING PERIOD
  In order to finance the Group’s operation, CR Power                  本公司全資附屬公司華潤電力投
  Investment, a wholly-owned subsidiary of the Company, has            資 發 行 兩 期 永 續 中 期 票 據，金 額 分
  issued two batches of perpetual medium-term notes amounting          別 為 人 民 幣 2,000,000,000 元（「 C
  to RMB2,000,000,000 (“Batch C”) and RMB2,000,000,000                 期」）及 人 民 幣 2,000,000,000 元（「 D
  (“Batch D”). The proceeds raised are designated for repaying         期」），以 為 本 集 團 營 運 提 供 資 金。
  the Group’s interest-bearing debts and supplementing working         籌集的所得款項擬用於償還本集團
  capital.                                                             計息債務及補充營運資金。

  For Batch C notes, the notes carry initial coupon rate (including    就 C 期票據而言，票據的初始票面利
  initial spread) of 1.97%. If the issuer chooses not to redeem        率（包 括 初 始 利 差）為 1.97%。倘 發
  the notes, the coupon rate will be adjusted to the current           行 人 選 擇 不 贖 回 該 票 據，則 票 面 利
  benchmark interest rate plus initial spread and premium of 150       率將自第六個計息年度起調整為當
  basis points from the beginning of the 6th interest-bearing          期 基 準 利 率 加 初 始 利 差 及 150 個 基
  year, and will remain unchanged from the 6th to 10th interest-       點 溢 價，且 於 第 六 至 第 十 個 計 息 年
  bearing years. The coupon rate will be reset following the same      度 期 間 保 持 不 變。此 後 每 五 年，票
  mechanism for every 5-year interval until the issuer exercises       面 利 率 將 按 相 同 機 制 重 新 設 定，直
  the redemption right.                                                至發行人行使贖回權為止。

  For Batch D notes, the notes carry initial coupon rate (including    就 D 期票據而言，票據的初始票面利
  initial spread) of 2.02%. If the issuer chooses not to redeem        率（包 括 初 始 利 差）為 2.02%。倘 發
  the notes, the coupon rate will be adjusted to the current           行 人 選 擇 不 贖 回 該 票 據，則 票 面 利
  benchmark interest rate plus initial spread and premium of 150       率將自第四個計息年度起調整為當
  basis points from the beginning of the 4th interest-bearing          期 基 準 利 率 加 初 始 利 差 及 150 個 基
  year, and will remain unchanged from the 4th to 6th interest-        點 溢 價，且 於 第 四 至 第 六 個 計 息 年
  bearing years. The coupon rate will be reset following the same      度 期 間 保 持 不 變。此 後 每 三 年，票
